{"lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"What colour are you going to use?","type":"example"},{"text":"What time is it?","type":"example"},{"text":"What kind of car is that?","type":"example"},{"text":"Did you buy the groceries? — With what money? We barely have any left.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Which, especially which of an open-ended set of possibilities."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wonder what colour he is going to use.","type":"example"},{"text":"I know what colour I am going to use.","type":"example"},{"text":"That depends on what answer is received.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Which."],"tags":["indirect","interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He seems to have lost what sense he had.","type":"example"},{"text":"What money I earn is soon spent.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any ... that; all ... that; whatever."],"tags":["relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This shows what beauty there is in nature.","type":"example"},{"text":"You know what nonsense she talks.","type":"example"},{"text":"I found out what a liar he is.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Emphasises that something is noteworthy or remarkable in quality or degree, in either a good or bad way; may be used in combination with certain other determiners, especially 'a', less often 'some'."]},{"examples":[{"text":"What nonsense!","type":"example"},{"text":"Wow! What a speech.","type":"example"},{"text":"What beautiful children you have.","type":"example"},{"text":"With what passion she sings!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Emphasises that something is noteworthy or remarkable in quality or degree, in either a good or bad way; may be used in combination with certain other determiners, especially 'a', less often 'some'.","Used to form exclamations indicating that something is remarkable, in either a good or bad way."],"synonyms":[{"word":"such"}]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"wŏt"},{"ipa":"/wɒt/","tags":["New-York-City","New-Zealand","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/wɔt/","tags":["Australia","British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/wʱɔʈ/","tags":["South-Asia"]},{"enpr":"wät"},{"ipa":"/wɑt/","tags":["US","sometimes"]},{"ipa":"/wɑt/","tags":["General-South-African"]},{"ipa":"/wɜt/","tags":["General-South-African"]},{"ipa":"/wʱɑʈ/","tags":["South-Asia"]},{"enpr":"hwŏt"},{"ipa":"/ʍɒt/"},{"ipa":"/ʍɔt/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"enpr":"wŭt","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US"]},{"ipa":"/wʌt/","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US"]},{"ipa":"[wɐ(ː)t̚˨˩]","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US"]},{"enpr":"hwŭt","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US"]},{"ipa":"/ʍʌt/","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US"]},{"ipa":"/hɒt/","tags":["Canada","Singapore","US","dialectal"]}],"upstream_line":17872,"upstream_offset":122553630,"upstream_record_sha256":"6d325f32c9e91ff7871df73b2b7ae338ed0fe6a40858b9d870d41b495e267ce8","word":"what"}
